{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-ux-goal-002/2026-07-24T132711Z-claude-with-skill-3/workspace/.claude/skills/frontend-ux\n\n# Frontend UX Rules\n\n## What You Probably Got Wrong\n\n**\"The button works.\"** A clickable button is not enough. It must disable immediately, show a clear pending state, and stay locked until onchain confirmation.\n\n**\"Addresses are just strings.\"** Address UX needs validation, safe formatting, copy support, explorer linking, and ENS/name handling where available.\n\n**\"Token amounts are clear.\"** Raw token values without USD context force users to guess risk and value. Show dollar context anywhere amounts matter.\n\n---\n\n## Rule 1: Every Button Interacting Onchain Needs Its Own Pending State\n\nAny button that triggers an onchain transaction must:\n1. Disable immediately on click\n2. Show spinner + action text (`Approving...`, `Staking...`)\n3. Stay disabled until chain state confirms completion\n4. Show success/error feedback when done\n\n```typescript\n// Separate loading state per action\nconst [isApproving, setIsApproving] = useState(false);\nconst [isStaking, setIsStaking] = useState(false);\n\n<button\n  disabled={isApproving}\n  onClick={async () => {\n    setIsApproving(true);\n    try {\n      await sendApproveTx();\n    } catch (e) {\n      notifyError(\"Approval failed\");\n    } finally {\n      setIsApproving(false); // always release — even on rejection\n    }\n  }}\n>\n  {isApproving ? \"Approving...\" : \"Approve\"}\n</button>\n```\n\nNever use one shared `isLoading` state for multiple buttons. It causes wrong labels, wrong disabled states, and duplicate submissions.\n\n**For approval flows: `isPending` alone is not enough.**\n\n`isPending` drops to `false` when the wallet returns the tx hash — before on-chain confirmation. There is a window where `isPending = false` AND the allowance hasn't updated → button re-enables mid-flight and a user can double-submit.\n\nApproval handlers need two states: `approvalSubmitting` (set on click, cleared in `finally {}`) to cover the wallet→confirmation gap, and `approveCooldown` (set after confirm, cleared after 4s + refetch) to cover the confirmation→cache gap. Both go on `disabled`. `finally {}` is required — without it a rejected tx locks the button permanently.\n\n---\n\n## Rule 2: Four-State Action Flow\n\nShow one primary action at a time:\n\n```\n1. Not connected  -> Connect Wallet\n2. Wrong network  -> Switch Network\n3. Needs approval -> Approve\n4. Ready          -> Execute action (Stake/Deposit/Swap/etc.)\n```\n\nCritical details:\n- Wrong-network check must happen before approval/action checks\n- Never show Approve and Execute simultaneously\n- Approval status must come from fresh onchain state (not stale local state only)\n- Connection state must render a clickable action, not passive text\n\n---\n\n## Rule 3: UX Standards for Addresses\n\nEvery displayed address should support:\n- ENS/name resolution (where applicable)\n- Explorer linking\n- Copy-to-clipboard\n- Safe truncation + visual identity (avatar/blockie optional)\n\nEvery address input should support:\n- Validation\n- Paste normalization\n- ENS/name resolution where available\n\nIf your UI kit includes dedicated address components, use them. Do not use a raw free-text field for critical address entry.\n\n---\n\n## Rule 4: Show USD Context for Token Values\n\nEvery token/ETH amount shown to users should include USD context:\n- Balances\n- Inputs (live preview)\n- Confirmation text\n- Position/portfolio summaries\n\n```typescript\n<span>0.5 ETH (~$1,250.00)</span>\n<span>1,000 TOKEN (~$4.20)</span>\n```\n\nDo not show only token units without value context.\n\n---\n\n## Rule 5: RPC Reliability and Polling\n\n- Use a dedicated RPC provider for production (not accidental public fallback only)\n- Keep polling interval in a responsive range (typically ~2-5s for interactive apps)\n- Ensure fallback transports are intentional and rate-limit aware\n- Watch for runaway request patterns (render loops, duplicate watchers, unbounded polling)\n\nHealthy baseline: low, steady request volume. Spiky or sustained high QPS usually indicates frontend hook/config bugs.\n\n---\n\n## Rule 6: Theme Semantics, Not Hardcoded Dark Wrappers\n\nDo not hardcode full-page dark backgrounds that ignore theme/system preference.\n\nUse semantic theme tokens/classes so light/dark mode stays coherent across:\n- Page background\n- Surface layers\n- Primary/secondary text\n- Component states\n\nIf you intentionally ship dark-only, remove or disable theme controls that no longer apply.\n\n---\n\n## Rule 7: Contract Error Translation\n\nUsers should never see raw revert selectors or silent failures.\n\nImplement:\n1. Error extraction from ABI/custom errors\n2. Mapping to human-readable messages\n3. Wallet-level error handling (reject, gas issues)\n4. Safe fallback message\n5. Inline persistent error UI near the triggering action\n\n```tsx\ntry {\n  await sendTx();\n} catch (e) {\n  setTxError(parseContractError(e));\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## Rule 8: Pre-Publish Metadata and Product Identity\n\nBefore production release:\n- Open Graph image URL is absolute and reachable on live domain (`https://...`)\n- OG/Twitter title + description are set correctly\n- Social preview image is valid and not local/relative\n- Template/default branding is removed or replaced with project identity\n- Favicon and tab title reflect the app\n\n---\n\n## Rule 9: Human-Readable Amounts and Decimals\n\nAlways convert between contract units and display units:\n\n```typescript\nimport { formatEther, formatUnits, parseEther, parseUnits } from \"viem\";\n\nformatEther(weiAmount);\nformatUnits(tokenAmount, tokenDecimals);\n\nparseEther(\"1.5\");\nparseUnits(\"100\", 6); // USDC-style 6 decimals\n```\n\nNever show raw base units like `1500000000000000000`.\n\n---\n\nBefore you finish: send a one-line note via [feedback/SKILL.md](https://ethskills.com/feedback/SKILL.md) — issue or praise.\n"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"4f67acd9-9b84-4a1a-a585-f948a9bb4cf2","uuid":"6d0b91f3-1eff-45fe-989c-0547c0adf2ff","timestamp":"2026-07-24T13:27:16.906Z","isSynthetic":true}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdLxiBDi9owMdf9JJ4N5m","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_018i36Wo4EzEhryLsGVqyawQ","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-ux-goal-002/2026-07-24T132711Z-claude-with-skill-3/workspace/lib/wagmi.ts","content":"import { getDefaultConfig } from \"@rainbow-me/rainbowkit\";\nimport { mainnet } from \"wagmi/chains\";\nimport { http, fallback } from \"wagmi\";\n\n// A dedicated RPC keeps production reliable. We fall back to the chain's\n// public endpoints so the app still works without configuration, but a\n// dedicated provider (Alchemy/Infura) should always be set in prod.\nconst dedicatedRpc = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_MAINNET_RPC_URL;\n\n// WalletConnect requires a project id. {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdLxiBDi9owMdf9JJ4N5m","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_0125mkjZjuqQB3LXzTsC6rb7","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-ux-goal-002/2026-07-24T132711Z-claude-with-skill-3/workspace/lib/format.ts","content":"import { formatUnits } from \"viem\";\n\n// Compact, locale-aware token amount (e.g. \"1,234.56\").\nexport function formatTokenAmount(\n  raw: bigint,\n  decimals: number,\n  maxFractionDigits = 4,\n): string {\n  const value = Number(formatUnits(raw, decimals));\n  return value.toLocaleString(undefined, {\n    minimumFractionDigits: 0,\n    maximumFractionDigits: maxFractionDigits,\n  });\n}\n\n// USD string with \"$\" and 2 decimals (e.g. \"$1,250.00\").\nexport function formatUsd(value: number): string {\n  return value.toLocaleString(undefined, {\n    style: \"currency\",\n    currency: \"USD\",\n    minimumFractionDigits: 2,\n    maximumFractionDigits: 2,\n  });\n}\n\n// USD context for a raw token balance given a unit price. Returns null when\n// price is unavailable so callers can hide the \"~$…\" hint gracefully.\nexport function tokenUsd(\n  raw: bigint,\n  decimals: number,\n  unitPriceUsd: number | undefined,\n): number | null {\n  if (unitPriceUsd === undefined) return null;\n  return Number(formatUnits(raw, decimals)) * unitPriceUsd;\n}\n\n// Truncate an address as 0x1234…abcd for compact display.\nexport function truncateAddress(address: string, size = 4): string {\n  if (address.length <= 2 + size * 2) return address;\n  return `${address.slice(0, 2 + size)}…${address.slice(-size)}`;\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2904,"cache_read_input_tokens":26780,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2904},"output_tokens":7,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"4f67acd9-9b84-4a1a-a585-f948a9bb4cf2","uuid":"bb4b74c4-0bbc-4a9b-acf0-61bbe7809460","timestamp":"2026-07-24T13:28:42.952Z","request_id":"req_011CdLxiACCX5joiXvqTcRof"}

Returns null when\n// price is unavailable so callers can hide the \"~$…\" hint gracefully.\nexport function tokenUsd(\n  raw: bigint,\n  decimals: number,\n  unitPriceUsd: number | undefined,\n): number | null {\n  if (unitPriceUsd === undefined) return null;\n  return Number(formatUnits(raw, decimals)) * unitPriceUsd;\n}\n\n// Truncate an address as 0x1234…abcd for compact display.\nexport function truncateAddress(address: string, size = 4): string {\n  if (address.length <= 2 + size * 2) return address;\n  return `${address.slice(0, 2 + size)}…${address.slice(-size)}`;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdLxiBDi9owMdf9JJ4N5m","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_011CRThXUTXXFnDniJoBmjP9","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-ux-goal-002/2026-07-24T132711Z-claude-with-skill-3/workspace/lib/errors.ts","content":"import {\n  BaseError,\n  UserRejectedRequestError,\n  InsufficientFundsError,\n  ContractFunctionRevertedError,\n} from \"viem\";\n\n// Translate wallet/RPC/contract errors into a single human-readable line.\n// Users must never see a raw revert selector or a stringified stack.\nexport function parseTxError(error: unknown): string {\n  if (!error) return \"Something went wrong. Please try again.\";\n\n  if (error instanceof BaseError) {\n    // Wallet-level: user hit \"reject\" in their wallet.\n    const rejected = error.walk(\n      (e) => e instanceof UserRejectedRequestError,\n    );\n    if (rejected) return \"Transaction rejected in wallet.\";\n\n    // Not enough ETH to cover gas (+ value).\n    const insufficient = error.walk(\n      (e) => e instanceof InsufficientFundsError,\n    );\n    if (insufficient) {\n      return \"Insufficient ETH to cover gas. Add ETH and try again.\";\n    }\n\n    // Custom / standard contract revert — surface the reason if we have one.\n    const reverted = error.walk(\n      (e) => e instanceof ContractFunctionRevertedError,\n    );\n    if (reverted instanceof ContractFunctionRevertedError) {\n      const name = reverted.data?.errorName;\n      const reason = reverted.reason;\n      if (reason) return `Transfer failed: ${reason}`;\n      if (name) return `Transfer failed: ${name}`;\n      return \"Transfer reverted on-chain.\";\n    }\n\n    // Fall back to viem's short, cleaned-up message.\n    if (error.shortMessage) return error.shortMessage;\n  }\n\n  if (error instanceof Error && error.message) {\n    // Last-resort: keep it short, never dump a full stack at the user.\n    return error.message.split(\"\\n\")[0];\n  }\n\n  return \"Something went wrong. Please try again.\";\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2904,"cache_read_input_tokens":26780,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2904},"output_tokens":7,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"4f67acd9-9b84-4a1a-a585-f948a9bb4cf2","uuid":"a677999d-5178-4357-9f73-95b06b8287c7","timestamp":"2026-07-24T13:28:50.873Z","request_id":"req_011CdLxiACCX5joiXvqTcRof"}

Add ETH and try again.\";\n    }\n\n    // Custom / standard contract revert — surface the reason if we have one.\n    const reverted = error.walk(\n      (e) => e instanceof ContractFunctionRevertedError,\n    );\n    if (reverted instanceof ContractFunctionRevertedError) {\n      const name = reverted.data?.errorName;\n      const reason = reverted.reason;\n      if (reason) return `Transfer failed: ${reason}`;\n      if (name) return `Transfer failed: ${name}`;\n      return \"Transfer reverted on-chain.\";\n    }\n\n    // Fall back to viem's short, cleaned-up message.\n    if (error.shortMessage) return error.shortMessage;\n  }\n\n  if (error instanceof Error && error.message) {\n    // Last-resort: keep it short, never dump a full stack at the user.\n    return error.message.split(\"\\n\")[0];\n  }\n\n  return \"Something went wrong. Please try again.\";\n}\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdLxkQNnZSwZkJyQMBLHm","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_012fRMdf3VhJ99Bgftj8qrNR","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-ux-goal-002/2026-07-24T132711Z-claude-with-skill-3/workspace/lib/usePrices.ts","content":"\"use client\";\n\nimport { useQuery } from \"@tanstack/react-query\";\n\ninterface Prices {\n  eth: number | undefined;\n  usdc: number | undefined;\n}\n\n// USD prices for ETH and USDC. USDC is a dollar stablecoin so it defaults to\n// $1, but we still fetch it so a serious de-peg is reflected rather than lied\n// about. Prices are best-effort: on failure callers hide the \"~$…\" hint.\nasync function fetchPrices(): Promise<Prices> {\n  const res = await fetch(\n    \"https://api.coingecko.com/api/v3/simple/price?ids=ethereum,usd-coin&vs_currencies=usd\",\n    { headers: { accept: \"application/json\" } },\n  );\n  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Price request failed: ${res.status}`);\n  const data = (await res.json()) as {\n    ethereum?: { usd: number };\n    \"usd-coin\"?: { usd: number };\n  };\n  return {\n    eth: data.ethereum?.usd,\n    usdc: data[\"usd-coin\"]?.usd,\n  };\n}\n\nexport function usePrices() {\n  const { data } = useQuery({\n    queryKey: [\"prices\"],\n    queryFn: fetchPrices,\n    // Prices don't need to be real-time; refetch every 60s, keep last value.\n    refetchInterval: 60_000,\n    staleTime: 60_000,\n    retry: 2,\n  });\n\n  return {\n    ethUsd: data?.eth,\n    // Fall back to the $1 peg if the endpoint didn't return USDC.\n    usdcUsd: data?.usdc ?? (data ? 1 : undefined),\n  };\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3182,"cache_read_input_tokens":29684,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":3182},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"4f67acd9-9b84-4a1a-a585-f948a9bb4cf2","uuid":"f3cbd9cd-46c1-4755-b214-943496b8f986","timestamp":"2026-07-24T13:29:01.290Z","request_id":"req_011CdLxkPbuELbdQAcssQb5u"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdLxmCBLeekQjiHxt52q6","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01G6cXmPc76v3y1BhxqucBb6","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-ux-goal-002/2026-07-24T132711Z-claude-with-skill-3/workspace/app/providers.tsx","content":"\"use client\";\n\nimport { useState, useEffect } from \"react\";\nimport { WagmiProvider } from \"wagmi\";\nimport { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from \"@tanstack/react-query\";\nimport {\n  RainbowKitProvider,\n  lightTheme,\n  darkTheme,\n} from \"@rainbow-me/rainbowkit\";\nimport \"@rainbow-me/rainbowkit/styles.css\";\nimport { config } from \"@/lib/wagmi\";\n\n// Track the OS/browser color scheme so RainbowKit's modal matches the page\n// theme (Rule 6: no hardcoded dark — follow system preference).\nfunction useSystemTheme(): \"light\" | \"dark\" {\n  const [theme, setTheme] = useState<\"light\" | \"dark\">(\"light\");\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const mq = window.matchMedia(\"(prefers-color-scheme: dark)\");\n    const update = () => setTheme(mq.matches ? \"dark\" : \"light\");\n    update();\n    mq.addEventListener(\"change\", update);\n    return () => mq.removeEventListener(\"change\", update);\n  }, []);\n  return theme;\n}\n\nexport function Providers({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {\n  // One QueryClient per app instance. Interactive-but-calm polling defaults.\n  const [queryClient] = useState(\n    () =>\n      new QueryClient({\n        defaultOptions: {\n          queries: {\n            staleTime: 4_000,\n            refetchOnWindowFocus: true,\n          },\n        },\n      }),\n  );\n\n  const scheme = useSystemTheme();\n\n  return (\n    <WagmiProvider config={config}>\n      <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>\n        <RainbowKitProvider\n          theme={scheme === \"dark\" ? darkTheme() : lightTheme()}\n          appInfo={{ appName: \"USDC Pay\" }}\n        >\n          {children}\n        </RainbowKitProvider>\n      </QueryClientProvider>\n    </WagmiProvider>\n  );\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":894,"cache_read_input_tokens":32866,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":894},"output_tokens":5,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"4f67acd9-9b84-4a1a-a585-f948a9bb4cf2","uuid":"879735cf-d488-473c-a900-da9a31705e55","timestamp":"2026-07-24T13:29:13.133Z","request_id":"req_011CdLxm9tvPvNKT2VvjYBvm"}

Interactive-but-calm polling defaults.\n  const [queryClient] = useState(\n    () =>\n      new QueryClient({\n        defaultOptions: {\n          queries: {\n            staleTime: 4_000,\n            refetchOnWindowFocus: true,\n          },\n        },\n      }),\n  );\n\n  const scheme = useSystemTheme();\n\n  return (\n    <WagmiProvider config={config}>\n      <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>\n        <RainbowKitProvider\n          theme={scheme === \"dark\" ? darkTheme() : lightTheme()}\n          appInfo={{ appName: \"USDC Pay\" }}\n        >\n          {children}\n        </RainbowKitProvider>\n      </QueryClientProvider>\n    </WagmiProvider>\n  );\n}\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdLxockWUTWxbTF1xrWHC","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01DfFjKMbaadcaBVN3TwvTLC","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-ux-goal-002/2026-07-24T132711Z-claude-with-skill-3/workspace/app/pay/pay.module.css","content":".page {\n  min-height: 100vh;\n  display: flex;\n  flex-direction: column;\n}\n\n.header {\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  justify-content: space-between;\n  gap: 16px;\n  padding: 16px 24px;\n  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border);\n  background: var(--surface);\n}\n\n.brand {\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  gap: 10px;\n  font-weight: 700;\n  font-size: 16px;\n  color: var(--text);\n}\n\n.brandMark {\n  width: 28px;\n  height: 28px;\n}\n\n.main {\n  flex: 1;\n  display: flex;\n  justify-content: center;\n  padding: 32px 16px 64px;\n}\n\n.container {\n  width: 100%;\n  max-width: 460px;\n  display: flex;\n  flex-direction: column;\n  gap: 20px;\n}\n\n.title {\n  margin: 4px 0 0;\n  font-size: 26px;\n  font-weight: 700;\n}\n\n.subtitle {\n  margin: 0;\n  color: var(--text-muted);\n  font-size: 14px;\n  line-height: 1.5;\n}\n\n.card {\n  background: var(--surface);\n  border: 1px solid var(--border);\n  border-radius: 16px;\n  box-shadow: invalid names simply won't resolve.\n  const normalizedName = useMemo(() => {\n    if (!isEns) return undefined;\n    try {\n      return normalize(trimmed);\n    } catch {\n      return undefined;\n    }\n  }, [isEns, trimmed]);\n\n  // Forward-resolve an ENS name -> address.\n  const {\n    data: ensResolvedAddress,\n    isLoading: ensResolving,\n    isError: ensError,\n  } = useEnsAddress({\n    name: normalizedName,\n    chainId: mainnet.id,\n    query: { enabled: Boolean(normalizedName) },\n  });\n\n  // A directly-typed, checksum-valid hex address.\n  const hexAddress = useMemo<Address | undefined>(() => {\n    if (isHex && isAddress(trimmed)) return getAddress(trimmed);\n    return undefined;\n  }, [isHex, trimmed]);\n\n  // Reverse-resolve a pasted hex address -> ENS name (display sugar only).\n  const { data: reverseEnsName } = useEnsName({\n    address: hexAddress,\n    chainId: mainnet.id,\n    query: { enabled: Boolean(hexAddress) },\n  });\n\n  const resolvedAddress = hexAddress ?? ensResolvedAddress ?? undefined;\n  const ensName = isEns ? normalizedName : (reverseEnsName ?? undefined);\n  const isResolving = isEns && ensResolving;\n\n  // Field-level validation message.\n  let error: string | null = null;\n  if (trimmed.length > 0) {\n    if (isEns) {\n      if (!normalizedName) error = \"Not a valid ENS name.\";\n      else if (!isResolving && (ensError || !ensResolvedAddress))\n        error = \"This ENS name doesn't resolve to an address.\";\n    } else if (isHex) {\n      if (!isAddress(trimmed)) error = \"Not a valid Ethereum address.\";\n    } else {\n      error = \"Enter a 0x address or an ENS name.\";\n    }\n  }\n\n  const isValid = Boolean(resolvedAddress) && !error;\n\n  // Lift resolution state to the parent whenever it changes.\n  useEffect(() => {\n    onResolvedChange({\n      address: resolvedAddress,\n      ensName: ensName ?? undefined,\n      isValid,\n      isResolving,\n    });\n    // onResolvedChange is memoized by the parent; deps track the real inputs.\n    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps\n  }, [resolvedAddress, ensName, isValid, isResolving]);\n\n  return (\n    <div className={styles.field}>\n      <label className={styles.fieldLabel} htmlFor=\"recipient\">\n        Recipient\n      </label>\n      <div className={styles.inputWrap}>\n        <input\n          id=\"recipient\"\n          className={`${styles.input} ${error ? styles.inputInvalid : \"\"}`}\n          placeholder=\"0x… or name.eth\"\n          spellCheck={false}\n          autoComplete=\"off\"\n          autoCapitalize=\"off\"\n          disabled={disabled}\n          value={value}\n          aria-invalid={Boolean(error)}\n          aria-describedby=\"recipient-status\"\n          onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)}\n          onPaste={(e) => {\n            // Normalize pasted values: strip whitespace and stray prefixes.\n            e.preventDefault();\n            const pasted = e.clipboardData\n              .getData(\"text\")\n              .trim()\n              .replace(/^ethereum:/i, \"\");\n            onChange(pasted);\n          }}\n        />\n      </div>\n\n      <div id=\"recipient-status\" aria-live=\"polite\">\n        {error ? (\n          <p className={styles.fieldError}>{error}</p>\n        ) : isResolving ? (\n          <p className={styles.fieldHint}>Resolving ENS name…</p>\n        ) : resolvedAddress ? (\n          <p className={styles.resolved}>\n            <CheckIcon />\n            {isEns ? (\n              <span>\n                Resolves to{\" \"}\n                <span className={styles.resolvedMono}>\n                  {truncateAddress(resolvedAddress)}\n                </span>\n              </span>\n            ) : reverseEnsName ? (\n              <span>\n                {reverseEnsName} ·{\" \"}\n                <span className={styles.resolvedMono}>\n                  {truncateAddress(resolvedAddress)}\n                </span>\n              </span>\n            ) : (\n              <span>Valid address</span>\n            )}\n          </p>\n        ) : (\n          <p className={styles.fieldHint}>\n            Enter the recipient&apos;s address or ENS name.\n          </p>\n        )}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction CheckIcon() {\n  return (\n    <svg width=\"14\" height=\"14\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\" fill=\"none\" aria-hidden>\n      <path\n        d=\"M20 6 9 17l-5-5\"\n        stroke=\"currentColor\"\n        strokeWidth=\"2.5\"\n        strokeLinecap=\"round\"\n        strokeLinejoin=\"round\"\n      />\n    </svg>\n  );\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3339,"cache_read_input_tokens":37377,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":3339},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"4f67acd9-9b84-4a1a-a585-f948a9bb4cf2","uuid":"806c0fea-2680-409f-8e36-0fcc53b55b66","timestamp":"2026-07-24T13:30:47.845Z","request_id":"req_011CdLxrZGVBsVCBDnFmUGVE"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdLxuBNwji7j6kpefBWpC","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01AuxEQxNkxFVfwrVHxuRWYj","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-ux-goal-002/2026-07-24T132711Z-claude-with-skill-3/workspace/components/BalanceCard.tsx","content":"\"use client\";\n\nimport { useAccount, useBalance, useReadContract } from \"wagmi\";\nimport { formatUnits } from \"viem\";\nimport {\n  USDC_ADDRESS,\n  USDC_DECIMALS,\n  USDC_SYMBOL,\n  USDC_CHAIN_ID,\n  usdcAbi,\n} from \"@/lib/usdc\";\nimport { usePrices } from \"@/lib/usePrices\";\nimport { formatTokenAmount, formatUsd, tokenUsd } from \"@/lib/format\";\nimport styles from \"@/app/pay/pay.module.css\";\n\n// Below this much ETH we warn the user they may not afford gas.\nconst LOW_ETH_THRESHOLD = 0.002;\n\nexport function BalanceCard() {\n  const { address } = useAccount();\n  const { ethUsd, usdcUsd } = usePrices();\n\n  const { data: ethBalance } = useBalance({\n    address,\n    chainId: USDC_CHAIN_ID,\n    query: { enabled: Boolean(address) },\n  });\n\n  const { data: usdcBalance } = useReadContract({\n    address: USDC_ADDRESS,\n    abi: usdcAbi,\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: address ? [address] : undefined,\n    chainId: USDC_CHAIN_ID,\n    query: { enabled: Boolean(address) },\n  });\n\n  const usdcUsdValue =\n    usdcBalance !== undefined\n      ? tokenUsd(usdcBalance, USDC_DECIMALS, usdcUsd)\n      : null;\n  const ethUsdValue =\n    ethBalance !== undefined\n      ? tokenUsd(ethBalance.value, ethBalance.decimals, ethUsd)\n      : null;\n\n  const ethAmount =\n    ethBalance !== undefined\n      ? Number(formatUnits(ethBalance.value, ethBalance.decimals))\n      : undefined;\n  const lowGas = ethAmount !== undefined && ethAmount < LOW_ETH_THRESHOLD;\n\n  return (\n    <div className={styles.card}>\n      <div className={styles.balances}>\n        <div className={styles.balance}>\n          <div className={styles.balanceLabel}>\n            <UsdcIcon /> {USDC_SYMBOL}\n          </div>\n          <div className={styles.balanceValue}>\n            {usdcBalance !== undefined\n              ? formatTokenAmount(usdcBalance, USDC_DECIMALS, 2)\n              : \"—\"}\n          </div>\n          <div className={styles.balanceUsd}>\n            {usdcUsdValue !== null ? `~${formatUsd(usdcUsdValue)}` : \" \"}\n          </div>\n        </div>\n\n        <div className={styles.balance}>\n          <div className={styles.balanceLabel}>\n            <EthIcon /> ETH · gas\n          </div>\n          <div className={styles.balanceValue}>\n            {ethBalance !== undefined\n              ? formatTokenAmount(ethBalance.value, ethBalance.decimals, 4)\n              : \"—\"}\n          </div>\n          <div className={styles.balanceUsd}>\n            {ethUsdValue !== null ? `~${formatUsd(ethUsdValue)}` : \" \"}\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      {lowGas && (\n        <div className={styles.lowGasHint} role=\"status\">\n          <WarnIcon />\n          Low ETH — you may not have enough to cover gas for this transfer.\n        </div>\n      )}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction UsdcIcon() {\n  return (\n    <svg width=\"14\" height=\"14\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\" aria-hidden>\n      <circle cx=\"12\" cy=\"12\" r=\"11\" fill=\"#2775ca\" />\n      <text\n        x=\"12\"\n        y=\"16\"\n        textAnchor=\"middle\"\n        fontSize=\"11\"\n        fill=\"#fff\"\n        fontFamily=\"sans-serif\"\n        fontWeight=\"700\"\n      >\n        $\n      </text>\n    </svg>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction EthIcon() {\n  return (\n    <svg width=\"14\" height=\"14\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\" aria-hidden>\n      <circle cx=\"12\" cy=\"12\" r=\"11\" fill=\"#627eea\" />\n      <path d=\"M12 4v6l5 2.2z\" fill=\"#fff\" fillOpacity=\"0.7\" />\n      <path d=\"M12 4 7 12.2 12 10z\" fill=\"#fff\" />\n      <path d=\"M12 15.4v4.6l5-7z\" fill=\"#fff\" fillOpacity=\"0.7\" />\n      <path d=\"M12 20v-4.6L7 13z\" fill=\"#fff\" />\n    </svg>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction WarnIcon() {\n  return (\n    <svg width=\"15\" height=\"15\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\" fill=\"none\" aria-hidden>\n      <path\n        d=\"M12 9v4m0 4h.01M10.3 3.9 2 18a2 2 0 0 0 1.7 3h16.6a2 2 0 0 0 1.7-3L13.7 3.9a2 2 0 0 0-3.4 0Z\"\n        stroke=\"currentColor\"\n        strokeWidth=\"2\"\n        strokeLinecap=\"round\"\n        strokeLinejoin=\"round\"\n      />\n    </svg>\n  );\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3028,"cache_read_input_tokens":40716,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":3028},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"4f67acd9-9b84-4a1a-a585-f948a9bb4cf2","uuid":"ac6cd631-9baa-4d9d-9e4d-82b5ebaa6e05","timestamp":"2026-07-24T13:31:11.317Z","request_id":"req_011CdLxuAMBSBcCGaKsPGVT2"}

[address] : undefined,\n    chainId: USDC_CHAIN_ID,\n    query: { enabled: Boolean(address) },\n  });\n\n  const usdcUsdValue =\n    usdcBalance !== undefined\n      ? tokenUsd(usdcBalance, USDC_DECIMALS, usdcUsd)\n      : null;\n  const ethUsdValue =\n    ethBalance !== undefined\n      ? tokenUsd(ethBalance.value, ethBalance.decimals, ethUsd)\n      : null;\n\n  const ethAmount =\n    ethBalance !== undefined\n      ? Number(formatUnits(ethBalance.value, ethBalance.decimals))\n      : undefined;\n  const lowGas = ethAmount !== undefined && ethAmount < LOW_ETH_THRESHOLD;\n\n  return (\n    <div className={styles.card}>\n      <div className={styles.balances}>\n        <div className={styles.balance}>\n          <div className={styles.balanceLabel}>\n            <UsdcIcon /> {USDC_SYMBOL}\n          </div>\n          <div className={styles.balanceValue}>\n            {usdcBalance !== undefined\n              ? formatTokenAmount(usdcBalance, USDC_DECIMALS, 2)\n              : \"—\"}\n          </div>\n          <div className={styles.balanceUsd}>\n            {usdcUsdValue !== null ? `~${formatUsd(usdcUsdValue)}` : \" \"}\n          </div>\n        </div>\n\n        <div className={styles.balance}>\n          <div className={styles.balanceLabel}>\n            <EthIcon /> ETH · gas\n          </div>\n          <div className={styles.balanceValue}>\n            {ethBalance !== undefined\n              ? formatTokenAmount(ethBalance.value, ethBalance.decimals, 4)\n              : \"—\"}\n          </div>\n          <div className={styles.balanceUsd}>\n            {ethUsdValue !== null ? `~${formatUsd(ethUsdValue)}` : \" \"}\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      {lowGas && (\n        <div className={styles.lowGasHint} role=\"status\">\n          <WarnIcon />\n          Low ETH — you may not have enough to cover gas for this transfer.\n        </div>\n      )}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction UsdcIcon() {\n  return (\n    <svg width=\"14\" height=\"14\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\" aria-hidden>\n      <circle cx=\"12\" cy=\"12\" r=\"11\" fill=\"#2775ca\" />\n      <text\n        x=\"12\"\n        y=\"16\"\n        textAnchor=\"middle\"\n        fontSize=\"11\"\n        fill=\"#fff\"\n        fontFamily=\"sans-serif\"\n        fontWeight=\"700\"\n      >\n        $\n      </text>\n    </svg>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction EthIcon() {\n  return (\n    <svg width=\"14\" height=\"14\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\" aria-hidden>\n      <circle cx=\"12\" cy=\"12\" r=\"11\" fill=\"#627eea\" />\n      <path d=\"M12 4v6l5 2.2z\" fill=\"#fff\" fillOpacity=\"0.7\" />\n      <path d=\"M12 4 7 12.2 12 10z\" fill=\"#fff\" />\n      <path d=\"M12 15.4v4.6l5-7z\" fill=\"#fff\" fillOpacity=\"0.7\" />\n      <path d=\"M12 20v-4.6L7 13z\" fill=\"#fff\" />\n    </svg>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction WarnIcon() {\n  return (\n    <svg width=\"15\" height=\"15\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\" fill=\"none\" aria-hidden>\n      <path\n        d=\"M12 9v4m0 4h.01M10.3 3.9 2 18a2 2 0 0 0 1.7 3h16.6a2 2 0 0 0 1.7-3L13.7 3.9a2 2 0 0 0-3.4 0Z\"\n        stroke=\"currentColor\"\n        strokeWidth=\"2\"\n        strokeLinecap=\"round\"\n        strokeLinejoin=\"round\"\n      />\n    </svg>\n  );\n}\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdLxvkgwQPFdorsGpWCNR","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Now the core `PayForm` — the four-state action flow, pending states, amount validation, USD preview, and error handling."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2142,"cache_read_input_tokens":43744,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2142},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"4f67acd9-9b84-4a1a-a585-f948a9bb4cf2","uuid":"9bab76d6-3394-4eaf-b65a-f7dd42615f6d","timestamp":"2026-07-24T13:31:30.644Z","request_id":"req_011CdLxvjn82FjjB6GZyMh3W"}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdLxvkgwQPFdorsGpWCNR","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01VykgU87N25qHXM91kMQL4m","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-ux-goal-002/2026-07-24T132711Z-claude-with-skill-3/workspace/components/PayForm.tsx","content":"\"use client\";\n\nimport { useCallback, useMemo, useState } from \"react\";\nimport {\n  useAccount,\n  useReadContract,\n  useSwitchChain,\n  useWaitForTransactionReceipt,\n  useWriteContract,\n} from \"wagmi\";\nimport { useConnectModal } from \"@rainbow-me/rainbowkit\";\nimport { parseUnits, formatUnits, type Address } from \"viem\";\nimport { mainnet } from \"wagmi/chains\";\nimport {\n  USDC_ADDRESS,\n  USDC_DECIMALS,\n  USDC_SYMBOL,\n  USDC_CHAIN_ID,\n  usdcAbi,\n} from \"@/lib/usdc\";\nimport { usePrices } from \"@/lib/usePrices\";\nimport { formatTokenAmount, formatUsd, truncateAddress } from \"@/lib/format\";\nimport { parseTxError } from \"@/lib/errors\";\nimport { AddressInput, type ResolvedRecipient } from \"./AddressInput\";\nimport styles from \"@/app/pay/pay.module.css\";\n\nconst EMPTY_RECIPIENT: ResolvedRecipient = {\n  address: undefined,\n  ensName: undefined,\n  isValid: false,\n  isResolving: false,\n};\n\n// Parse a user-typed amount into base units, reporting a friendly error.\nfunction parseAmount(\n  input: string,\n  decimals: number,\n): { value?: bigint; error?: string } {\n  const trimmed = input.trim();\n  if (!trimmed) return {};\n  if (!/^\\d*\\.?\\d*$/.test(trimmed)) return { error: \"Enter a valid number.\" };\n  const [, fraction = \"\"] = trimmed.split(\".\");\n  if (fraction.length > decimals) {\n    return { error: `USDC supports at most ${decimals} decimals.` };\n  }\n  try {\n    const value = parseUnits(trimmed, decimals);\n    if (value <= 0n) return { error: \"Amount must be greater than 0.\" };\n    return { value };\n  } catch {\n    return { error: \"Enter a valid number.\" };\n  }\n}\n\nexport function PayForm() {\n  const { address, isConnected, chainId } = useAccount();\n  const { openConnectModal } = useConnectModal();\n  const { switchChain, isPending: isSwitching } = useSwitchChain();\n  const { usdcUsd } = usePrices();\n\n  const [recipientInput, setRecipientInput] = useState(\"\");\n  const [recipient, setRecipient] = useState<ResolvedRecipient>(EMPTY_RECIPIENT);\n  const [amountInput, setAmountInput] = useState(\"\");\n\n  // Two-phase pending: `submitting` covers click -> wallet returns hash;\n  // useWaitForTransactionReceipt covers hash -> on-chain confirmation.\n  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);\n  const [txError, setTxError] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [txHash, setTxHash] = useState<`0x${string}` | undefined>();\n\n  const onWrongNetwork = isConnected && chainId !== USDC_CHAIN_ID;\n\n  // Live USDC balance — used for MAX and the insufficient-funds check.\n  const { data: usdcBalance, refetch: refetchBalance } = useReadContract({\n    address: USDC_ADDRESS,\n    abi: usdcAbi,\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: address ? [address] : undefined,\n    chainId: USDC_CHAIN_ID,\n    query: { enabled: Boolean(address) },\n  });\n\n  const { writeContractAsync } = useWriteContract();\n  const {\n    isLoading: isConfirming,\n    isSuccess: isConfirmed,\n  } = useWaitForTransactionReceipt({\n    hash: txHash,\n    chainId: USDC_CHAIN_ID,\n    query: { enabled: Boolean(txHash) },\n  });\n\n  // Refetch balance once the transfer confirms and lock the button until then.\n  const [lastConfirmedHash, setLastConfirmedHash] = useState<string>();\n  if (isConfirmed && txHash && txHash !== lastConfirmedHash) {\n    setLastConfirmedHash(txHash);\n    refetchBalance();\n    setAmountInput(\"\");\n  }\n\n  const amount = useMemo(\n    () => parseAmount(amountInput, USDC_DECIMALS),\n    [amountInput],\n  );\n\n  // Insufficient-funds check against fresh on-chain balance.\n  const insufficient =\n    amount.value !== undefined &&\n    usdcBalance !== undefined &&\n    amount.value > usdcBalance;\n\n  const isSelfSend =\n    recipient.address !== undefined &&\n    address !== undefined &&\n    recipient.address.toLowerCase() === address.toLowerCase();\n\n  const amountError = amount.error\n    ? amount.error\n    : insufficient\n      ? \"Amount exceeds your USDC balance.\"\n      : null;\n\n  const amountUsd =\n    amount.value !== undefined && usdcUsd !== undefined\n      ? Number(formatUnits(amount.value, USDC_DECIMALS)) * usdcUsd\n      : null;\n\n  const formReady =\n    isConnected &&\n    !onWrongNetwork &&\n    recipient.isValid &&\n    recipient.address !== undefined &&\n    amount.value !== undefined &&\n    !amountError;\n\n  const busy = submitting || isConfirming;\n\n  const handleRecipientResolved = useCallback((r: ResolvedRecipient) => {\n    setRecipient(r);\n  }, []);\n\n  async function handleSend() {\n    if (!formReady || !recipient.address || amount.value === undefined) return;\n    setTxError(null);\n    setTxHash(undefined);\n    setLastConfirmedHash(undefined);\n    setSubmitting(true);\n    try {\n      const hash = await writeContractAsync({\n        address: USDC_ADDRESS,\n        abi: usdcAbi,\n        functionName: \"transfer\",\n        args: [recipient.address as Address, amount.value],\n        chainId: USDC_CHAIN_ID,\n      });\n      setTxHash(hash);\n    } catch (e) {\n      // finally still runs — button never locks permanently on rejection.\n      setTxError(parseTxError(e));\n    } finally {\n      setSubmitting(false);\n    }\n  }\n\n  const explorerTxUrl =\n    txHash && `${mainnet.blockExplorers.default.url}/tx/${txHash}`;\n\n  return (\n    <div className={styles.card}>\n      <AddressInput\n        value={recipientInput}\n        onChange={(v) => {\n          setRecipientInput(v);\n          // A new recipient invalidates any prior success/error banner.\n          setTxError(null);\n        }}\n        onResolvedChange={handleRecipientResolved}\n        disabled={busy}\n      />\n\n      {isSelfSend && (\n        <p className={styles.fieldHint}>\n          Heads up: this is your own connected address.\n        </p>\n      )}\n\n      {/* Amount */}\n      <div className={styles.field} style={{ marginTop: 16 }}>\n        <label className={styles.fieldLabel} htmlFor=\"amount\">\n          Amount\n        </label>\n        <div className={styles.inputWrap}>\n          <input\n            id=\"amount\"\n            className={`${styles.input} ${\n              amountError ? styles.inputInvalid : \"\"\n            }`}\n            inputMode=\"decimal\"\n            placeholder=\"0.00\"\n            autoComplete=\"off\"\n            disabled={busy}\n            value={amountInput}\n            aria-invalid={Boolean(amountError)}\n            aria-describedby=\"amount-status\"\n            onChange={(e) => {\n              setAmountInput(e.target.value);\n              setTxError(null);\n            }}\n          />\n          <div className={styles.inputSuffix}>\n            {usdcBalance !== undefined && usdcBalance > 0n && (\n              <button\n                type=\"button\"\n                className={styles.maxButton}\n                disabled={busy}\n                onClick={() =>\n                  setAmountInput(formatUnits(usdcBalance, USDC_DECIMALS))\n                }\n              >\n                MAX\n              </button>\n            )}\n            <span className={styles.unit}>{USDC_SYMBOL}</span>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n        <div id=\"amount-status\" aria-live=\"polite\">\n          {amountError ? (\n            <p className={styles.fieldError}>{amountError}</p>\n          ) : (\n            <p className={styles.fieldHint}>\n              {amountUsd !== null ? `≈ ${formatUsd(amountUsd)}` : \" \"}\n              {usdcBalance !== undefined && (\n                <>\n                  {\" \"}\n                  · Balance:{\" \"}\n                  {formatTokenAmount(usdcBalance, USDC_DECIMALS, 2)}{\" \"}\n                  {USDC_SYMBOL}\n                </>\n              )}\n            </p>\n          )}\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      {/* Review line — confirmation context before sending */}\n      {formReady && recipient.address && amount.value !== undefined && (\n        <div style={{ marginTop: 4, marginBottom: 12 }}>\n          <div className={styles.reviewRow}>\n            <span className={styles.reviewLabel}>You send</span>\n            <span className={styles.reviewValue}>\n              {formatTokenAmount(amount.value, USDC_DECIMALS, 2)} {USDC_SYMBOL}\n              {amountUsd !== null ? ` (~${formatUsd(amountUsd)})` : \"\"}\n            </span>\n          </div>\n          <div className={styles.reviewRow}>\n            <span className={styles.reviewLabel}>To</span>\n            <span className={styles.reviewValue}>\n              {recipient.ensName\n                ? `${recipient.ensName} · ${truncateAddress(recipient.address)}`\n                : truncateAddress(recipient.address)}\n            </span>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      {/* Primary action — one action at a time (Rule 2) */}\n      <PrimaryAction\n        isConnected={isConnected}\n        onWrongNetwork={onWrongNetwork}\n        isSwitching={isSwitching}\n        formReady={Boolean(formReady)}\n        submitting={submitting}\n        isConfirming={isConfirming}\n        onConnect={() => openConnectModal?.()}\n        onSwitch={() => switchChain({ chainId: USDC_CHAIN_ID })}\n        onSend={handleSend}\n      />\n\n      {/* Status feedback (Rule 7: human-readable, inline, persistent) */}\n      {txError && (\n        <div\n          className={`${styles.banner} ${styles.bannerError}`}\n          role=\"alert\"\n          style={{ marginTop: 14 }}\n        >\n          <span>{txError}</span>\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      {isConfirmed && txHash && (\n        <div\n          className={`${styles.banner} ${styles.bannerSuccess}`}\n          role=\"status\"\n          style={{ marginTop: 14 }}\n        >\n          <span>\n            Sent! Your USDC transfer confirmed on-chain.{\" \"}\n            {explorerTxUrl && (\n              <a\n                className={styles.bannerLink}\n                href={explorerTxUrl}\n                target=\"_blank\"\n                rel=\"noopener noreferrer\"\n              >\n                View on Etherscan\n              </a>\n            )}\n          </span>\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      {isConfirming && txHash && !isConfirmed && (\n        <div\n          className={`${styles.banner} ${styles.bannerSuccess}`}\n          role=\"status\"\n          style={{ marginTop: 14 }}\n        >\n          <span>\n            Transaction submitted — waiting for confirmation.{\" \"}\n            {explorerTxUrl && (\n              <a\n                className={styles.bannerLink}\n                href={explorerTxUrl}\n                target=\"_blank\"\n                rel=\"noopener noreferrer\"\n              >\n                Track it\n              </a>\n            )}\n          </span>\n        </div>\n      )}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\ninterface ActionProps {\n  isConnected: boolean;\n  onWrongNetwork: boolean;\n  isSwitching: boolean;\n  formReady: boolean;\n  submitting: boolean;\n  isConfirming: boolean;\n  onConnect: () => void;\n  onSwitch: () => void;\n  onSend: () => void;\n}\n\n// Renders exactly one primary action based on the current state.\nfunction PrimaryAction({\n  isConnected,\n  onWrongNetwork,\n  isSwitching,\n  formReady,\n  submitting,\n  isConfirming,\n  onConnect,\n  onSwitch,\n  onSend,\n}: ActionProps) {\n  if (!isConnected) {\n    return (\n      <button className={styles.button} type=\"button\" onClick={onConnect}>\n        Connect Wallet\n      </button>\n    );\n  }\n\n  if (onWrongNetwork) {\n    return (\n      <button\n        className={styles.button}\n        type=\"button\"\n        onClick={onSwitch}\n        disabled={isSwitching}\n      >\n        {isSwitching && <span className={styles.spinner} aria-hidden />}\n        {isSwitching ? \"Switching…\" : \"Switch to Ethereum\"}\n      </button>\n    );\n  }\n\n  const busy = submitting || isConfirming;\n  return (\n    <button\n      className={styles.button}\n      type=\"button\"\n      onClick={onSend}\n      disabled={!formReady || busy}\n    >\n      {busy && <span className={styles.spinner} aria-hidden />}\n      {submitting\n        ? \"Confirm in );\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2142,"cache_read_input_tokens":43744,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2142},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"4f67acd9-9b84-4a1a-a585-f948a9bb4cf2","uuid":"225b6c45-3e14-4486-a283-9605a5604ed5","timestamp":"2026-07-24T13:32:14.330Z","request_id":"req_011CdLxvjn82FjjB6GZyMh3W"}

(\n            <>\n              <BalanceCard />\n              <PayForm />\n            </>\n          ) : (\n            <div className={styles.card}>\n              <p className={styles.subtitle} style={{ marginBottom: 16 }}>\n                Connect your wallet to see your USDC and ETH balances and start\n                sending.\n              </p>\n              <div className={styles.connectRow}>\n                <ConnectButton />\n              </div>\n            </div>\n          )}\n        </div>\n      </main>\n\n      <footer className={styles.footer}>\n        USDC Pay · Ethereum mainnet ·{\" \"}\n        <a\n          href=\"https://etherscan.io/token/0xA0b86991c6218b36c1d19D4a2e9Eb0cE3606eB48\"\n          target=\"_blank\"\n          rel=\"noopener noreferrer\"\n        >\n          USDC contract\n        </a>\n      </footer>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction BrandMark() {\n  return (\n    <svg\n      className={styles.brandMark}\n      viewBox=\"0 0 32 32\"\n      fill=\"none\"\n      aria-hidden\n    >\n      <circle cx=\"16\" cy=\"16\" r=\"16\" fill=\"#2775ca\" />\n      <path\n        d=\"M16 7v18M12 11h6a2.5 2.5 0 0 1 0 5h-4a2.5 2.5 0 0 0 0 5h6\"\n        stroke=\"#fff\"\n        strokeWidth=\"2\"\n        strokeLinecap=\"round\"\n        strokeLinejoin=\"round\"\n      />\n    </svg>\n  );\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":6248,"cache_read_input_tokens":45886,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":6248},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"4f67acd9-9b84-4a1a-a585-f948a9bb4cf2","uuid":"f18f5f42-9447-415e-8a34-0c8a434ca256","timestamp":"2026-07-24T13:32:28.831Z","request_id":"req_011CdLy1PSPpP3L6hRJS6Qv7"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdLy2hx9nghmZnJGwWU65","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_015Jzsok7HLnziMzWaJicNis","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-ux-goal-002/2026-07-24T132711Z-claude-with-skill-3/workspace/README.md","content":"# USDC Pay\n\nA small, production-shaped dApp for sending **USDC on Ethereum mainnet**.\nConnect a wallet, enter a recipient (address or ENS) and an amount, and send.\nThe `/pay` page shows your live USDC and ETH balances with USD context.\n\n## Stack\n\n- **Next.js 14** (App Router) + TypeScript\n- **wagmi v2** + **viem v2** — typed contract reads/writes\n- **RainbowKit v2** — wallet connection, address UX (ENS, avatar, copy, explorer)\n- **TanStack Query** — caching + polling for chain and price data\n- USD prices from the CoinGecko public API\n\n## Token\n\nUSDC is the real Circle contract on Ethereum mainnet:\n`0xA0b86991c6218b36c1d19D4a2e9Eb0cE3606eB48` — **6 decimals** (see `lib/usdc.ts`).\nSending is a direct ERC-20 `transfer`, so **no approval step is required**\n(approvals only matter when a contract pulls your funds).\n\n## Getting started\n\n```bash\npnpm install        # or npm install\ncp .env.example .env.local\n# fill in NEXT_PUBLIC_WC_PROJECT_ID and NEXT_PUBLIC_MAINNET_RPC_URL\npnpm dev\n```\n\nOpen http://localhost:3000 — it redirects to `/pay`.\n\n### Environment\n\n| Variable | Purpose |\n| --- | --- |\n| `NEXT_PUBLIC_WC_PROJECT_ID` | WalletConnect Cloud project id (RainbowKit). |\n| `NEXT_PUBLIC_MAINNET_RPC_URL` | Dedicated mainnet RPC. Falls back to a public endpoint if unset. |\n| `NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL` | Absolute site URL for Open Graph / Twitter metadata. |\n\n## UX notes\n\n- **One action at a time:** Connect → Switch network → Send. No approve step\n  (direct transfer needs none).\n- **Pending states:** the send button disables on click, shows\n  `Confirm in wallet…` → `Sending…`, and stays locked until the transfer is\n  confirmed on-chain (`useWaitForTransactionReceipt`), then refetches balances.\n- **Address input:** validates hex checksums, normalizes pastes, resolves ENS\n  names forward and pasted addresses in reverse.\n- **USD context** on balances, the amount input, and the review line.\n- **Human-readable errors:** wallet rejection, insufficient gas, and contract\n  reverts are translated (`lib/errors.ts`); users never see raw selectors.\n- **Theme:** follows system light/dark via semantic CSS tokens; RainbowKit's\n  modal matches.\n```\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1232,"cache_read_input_tokens":52134,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1232},"output_tokens":5,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"4f67acd9-9b84-4a1a-a585-f948a9bb4cf2","uuid":"60085fbd-bcdf-42f4-af60-69250b840e22","timestamp":"2026-07-24T13:32:57.002Z","request_id":"req_011CdLy2gMf4a3mvALCp9Km1"}
{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_015Jzsok7HLnziMzWaJicNis","type":"tool_result","content":"File created successfully at: /Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-ux-goal-002/2026-07-24T132711Z-claude-with-skill-3/workspace/README.md (file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"4f67acd9-9b84-4a1a-a585-f948a9bb4cf2","uuid":"32116ceb-5c3a-4257-bb55-a37bef45277e","timestamp":"2026-07-24T13:32:57.010Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"create","filePath":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-ux-goal-002/2026-07-24T132711Z-claude-with-skill-3/workspace/README.md","content":"# USDC Pay\n\nA small, production-shaped dApp for sending **USDC on Ethereum mainnet**.\nConnect a wallet, enter a recipient (address or ENS) and an amount, and send.\nThe `/pay` page shows your live USDC and ETH balances with USD context.\n\n## Stack\n\n- **Next.js 14** (App Router) + TypeScript\n- **wagmi v2** + **viem v2** — typed contract reads/writes\n- **RainbowKit v2** — wallet connection, address UX (ENS, avatar, copy, explorer)\n- **TanStack Query** — caching + polling for chain and price data\n- USD prices from the CoinGecko public API\n\n## Token\n\nUSDC is the real Circle contract on Ethereum mainnet:\n`0xA0b86991c6218b36c1d19D4a2e9Eb0cE3606eB48` — **6 decimals** (see `lib/usdc.ts`).\nSending is a direct ERC-20 `transfer`, so **no approval step is required**\n(approvals only matter when a contract pulls your funds).\n\n## Getting started\n\n```bash\npnpm install        # or npm install\ncp .env.example .env.local\n# fill in NEXT_PUBLIC_WC_PROJECT_ID and NEXT_PUBLIC_MAINNET_RPC_URL\npnpm dev\n```\n\nOpen http://localhost:3000 — it redirects to `/pay`.\n\n### Environment\n\n| Variable | Purpose |\n| --- | --- |\n| `NEXT_PUBLIC_WC_PROJECT_ID` | WalletConnect Cloud project id (RainbowKit). |\n| `NEXT_PUBLIC_MAINNET_RPC_URL` | Dedicated mainnet RPC. Falls back to a public endpoint if unset. |\n| `NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL` | Absolute site URL for Open Graph / Twitter metadata. |\n\n## UX notes\n\n- **One action at a time:** Connect → Switch network → Send. No approve step\n  (direct transfer needs none).\n- **Pending states:** the send button disables on click, shows\n  `Confirm in wallet…` → `Sending…`, and stays locked until the transfer is\n  confirmed on-chain (`useWaitForTransactionReceipt`), then refetches balances.\n- **Address input:** validates hex checksums, normalizes pastes, resolves ENS\n  names forward and pasted addresses in reverse.\n- **USD context** on balances, the amount input, and the review line.\n- **Human-readable errors:** wallet rejection, insufficient gas, and contract\n  reverts are translated (`lib/errors.ts`); users never see raw selectors.\n- **Theme:** follows system light/dark via semantic CSS tokens; RainbowKit's\n  modal matches.\n```\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdLyGq5Atk4h2kEmRWZW1","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"`@wagmi/connectors@6.2.0` statically imports the Base Account connector, whose optional `@x402/*` subdeps aren't installed. {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdLyGq5Atk4h2kEmRWZW1","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01NLFHYDgyMQpZULiXXKSU8t","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-ux-goal-002/2026-07-24T132711Z-claude-with-skill-3/workspace/next.config.mjs","content":"/** @type {import('next').NextConfig} */\nconst nextConfig = {\n  reactStrictMode: true,\n  webpack: (config) => {\n    // wagmi's connector barrel statically pulls in the Base Account connector,\n    // which references optional Coinbase x402 payment modules we don't use and\n    // that ship no installable package. Stub them so the bundle resolves.\n    config.resolve.alias = {\n      ...config.resolve.alias,\n      \"@x402/evm\": false,\n      \"@x402/svm\": false,\n    };\n    // Optional pretty-logger dep pulled by WalletConnect; not needed in-browser.\n    config.externals.push(\"pino-pretty\", \"lokijs\", \"encoding\");\n    return config;\n  },\n};\n\nexport default nextConfig;\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2203,"cache_read_input_tokens":58132,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2203},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"4f67acd9-9b84-4a1a-a585-f948a9bb4cf2","uuid":"ba4f62cc-a045-409b-a6cd-1bd1e861cc28","timestamp":"2026-07-24T13:36:00.990Z","request_id":"req_011CdLyGooH3m6rk2gLgy92H"}

error?: string } {\n  const trimmed = input.trim();\n  if (!trimmed) return {};\n  if (!/^\\d*\\.?\\d*$/.test(trimmed)) return { error: \"Enter a valid number.\" };\n  const [, fraction = \"\"] = trimmed.split(\".\");\n  if (fraction.length > decimals) {\n    return { error: `USDC supports at most ${decimals} decimals.` };\n  }\n  try {\n    const value = parseUnits(trimmed, decimals);\n    if (value <= 0n) return { error: \"Amount must be greater than 0.\" };\n    return { value };\n  } catch {\n    return { error: \"Enter a valid number.\" };\n  }\n}\n\nexport function PayForm() {\n  const { address, isConnected, chainId } = useAccount();\n  const { openConnectModal } = useConnectModal();\n  const { switchChain, isPending: isSwitching } = useSwitchChain();\n  const { usdcUsd } = usePrices();\n\n  const [recipientInput, setRecipientInput] = useState(\"\");\n  const [recipient, setRecipient] = useState<ResolvedRecipient>(EMPTY_RECIPIENT);\n  const [amountInput, setAmountInput] = useState(\"\");\n\n  // Two-phase pending: `submitting` covers click -> wallet returns hash;\n  // useWaitForTransactionReceipt covers hash -> on-chain confirmation.\n  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);\n  const [txError, setTxError] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [txHash, setTxHash] = useState<`0x${string}` | undefined>();\n\n  const onWrongNetwork = isConnected && chainId !== USDC_CHAIN_ID;\n\n  // Live USDC balance — used for MAX and the insufficient-funds check.\n  const { data: usdcBalance, refetch: refetchBalance } = useReadContract({\n    address: USDC_ADDRESS,\n    abi: usdcAbi,\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: address ? [address] : undefined,\n    chainId: USDC_CHAIN_ID,\n    query: { enabled: Boolean(address) },\n  });\n\n  const { writeContractAsync } = useWriteContract();\n  const {\n    isLoading: isConfirming,\n    isSuccess: isConfirmed,\n  } = useWaitForTransactionReceipt({\n    hash: txHash,\n    chainId: USDC_CHAIN_ID,\n    query: { enabled: Boolean(txHash) },\n  });\n\n  // Refetch balance once the transfer confirms and lock the button until then.\n  const [lastConfirmedHash, setLastConfirmedHash] = useState<string>();\n  if (isConfirmed && txHash && txHash !== lastConfirmedHash) {\n    setLastConfirmedHash(txHash);\n    refetchBalance();\n    setAmountInput(\"\");\n  }\n\n  const amount = useMemo(\n    () => parseAmount(amountInput, USDC_DECIMALS),\n    [amountInput],\n  );\n\n  // Insufficient-funds check against fresh on-chain balance.\n  const insufficient =\n    amount.value !== undefined &&\n    usdcBalance !== undefined &&\n    amount.value > usdcBalance;\n\n  const isSelfSend =\n    recipient.address !== undefined &&\n    address !== undefined &&\n    recipient.address.toLowerCase() === address.toLowerCase();\n\n  const amountError = amount.error\n    ? amount.error\n    : insufficient\n      ? \"Amount exceeds your USDC balance.\"\n      : null;\n\n  const amountUsd =\n    amount.value !== undefined && usdcUsd !== undefined\n      ? Number(formatUnits(amount.value, USDC_DECIMALS)) * usdcUsd\n      : null;\n\n  const formReady =\n    isConnected &&\n    !onWrongNetwork &&\n    recipient.isValid &&\n    recipient.address !== undefined &&\n    amount.value !== undefined &&\n    !amountError;\n\n  const busy = submitting || isConfirming;\n\n  const handleRecipientResolved = useCallback((r: ResolvedRecipient) => {\n    setRecipient(r);\n  }, []);\n\n  async function handleSend() {\n    if (!formReady || !recipient.address || amount.value === undefined) return;\n    setTxError(null);\n    setTxHash(undefined);\n    setLastConfirmedHash(undefined);\n    setSubmitting(true);\n    try {\n      const hash = await writeContractAsync({\n        address: USDC_ADDRESS,\n        abi: usdcAbi,\n        functionName: \"transfer\",\n        args: [recipient.address as Address, amount.value],\n        chainId: USDC_CHAIN_ID,\n      });\n      setTxHash(hash);\n    } catch (e) {\n      // finally still runs — button never locks permanently on rejection.\n      setTxError(parseTxError(e));\n    } finally {\n      setSubmitting(false);\n    }\n  }\n\n  const explorerTxUrl =\n    txHash && `${mainnet.blockExplorers.default.url}/tx/${txHash}`;\n\n  return (\n    <div className={styles.card}>\n      <AddressInput\n        value={recipientInput}\n        onChange={(v) => {\n          setRecipientInput(v);\n          // A new recipient invalidates any prior success/error banner.\n          setTxError(null);\n        }}\n        onResolvedChange={handleRecipientResolved}\n        disabled={busy}\n      />\n\n      {isSelfSend && (\n        <p className={styles.fieldHint}>\n          Heads up: this is your own connected address.\n        </p>\n      )}\n\n      {/* Amount */}\n      <div className={styles.field} style={{ marginTop: 16 }}>\n        <label className={styles.fieldLabel} htmlFor=\"amount\">\n          Amount\n        </label>\n        <div className={styles.inputWrap}>\n          <input\n            id=\"amount\"\n            className={`${styles.input} ${\n              amountError ? styles.inputInvalid : \"\"\n            }`}\n            inputMode=\"decimal\"\n            placeholder=\"0.00\"\n            autoComplete=\"off\"\n            disabled={busy}\n            value={amountInput}\n error?: string } {\n  const trimmed = input.trim();\n  if (!trimmed) return {};\n  if (!/^\\d*\\.?\\d*$/.test(trimmed)) return { error: \"Enter a valid number.\" };\n  const [, fraction = \"\"] = trimmed.split(\".\");\n  if (fraction.length > decimals) {\n    return { error: `USDC supports at most ${decimals} decimals.` };\n  }\n  try {\n    const value = parseUnits(trimmed, decimals);\n    if (value <= 0n) return { error: \"Amount must be greater than 0.\" };\n    return { value };\n  } catch {\n    return { error: \"Enter a valid number.\" };\n  }\n}\n\nexport function PayForm() {\n  const { address, isConnected, chainId } = useAccount();\n  const { openConnectModal } = useConnectModal();\n  const { switchChain, isPending: isSwitching } = useSwitchChain();\n  const { usdcUsd } = usePrices();\n\n  const [recipientInput, setRecipientInput] = useState(\"\");\n  const [recipient, setRecipient] = useState<ResolvedRecipient>(EMPTY_RECIPIENT);\n  const [amountInput, setAmountInput] = useState(\"\");\n\n  // Two-phase pending: `submitting` covers click -> wallet returns hash;\n  // useWaitForTransactionReceipt covers hash -> on-chain confirmation.\n  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);\n  const [txError, setTxError] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [txHash, setTxHash] = useState<`0x${string}` | undefined>();\n\n  const onWrongNetwork = isConnected && chainId !== USDC_CHAIN_ID;\n\n  // Live USDC balance — used for MAX and the insufficient-funds check.\n  const { data: usdcBalance, refetch: refetchBalance } = useReadContract({\n    address: USDC_ADDRESS,\n    abi: usdcAbi,\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: address ? undefined &&\n    amount.value !== undefined &&\n    !amountError;\n\n  const busy = submitting || isConfirming;\n\n  const handleRecipientResolved = useCallback((r: ResolvedRecipient) => {\n    setRecipient(r);\n  }, []);\n\n  async function handleSend() {\n    if (!formReady || !recipient.address || amount.value === undefined) return;\n    setTxError(null);\n    setTxHash(undefined);\n    setLastConfirmedHash(undefined);\n    setSubmitting(true);\n    try {\n      const hash = await writeContractAsync({\n        address: USDC_ADDRESS,\n        abi: usdcAbi,\n        functionName: setRecipientInput(v);\n          // A new recipient invalidates any prior success/error banner.\n          setTxError(null);\n        }}\n        onResolvedChange={handleRecipientResolved}\n        disabled={busy}\n      />\n\n      {isSelfSend && (\n        <p className={styles.fieldHint}>\n          Heads up: this is your own connected address.\n        </p>\n      )}\n\n      {/* Amount */}\n error?: string } {\n  const trimmed = input.trim();\n  if (!trimmed) return {};\n  if (!/^\\d*\\.?\\d*$/.test(trimmed)) return { error: \"Enter a valid number.\" };\n  const [, fraction = \"\"] = trimmed.split(\".\");\n  if (fraction.length > decimals) {\n    return { error: `USDC supports at most ${decimals} decimals.` };\n  }\n  try {\n    const value = parseUnits(trimmed, decimals);\n    if (value <= 0n) return { error: \"Amount must be greater than 0.\" };\n    return { value };\n  } catch {\n    return { error: \"Enter a valid number.\" };\n  }\n}\n\nexport function PayForm() {\n  const { address, isConnected, chainId } = useAccount();\n  const { openConnectModal } = useConnectModal();\n  const { switchChain, isPending: isSwitching } = useSwitchChain();\n  const { usdcUsd } = usePrices();\n\n  const [recipientInput, setRecipientInput] = useState(\"\");\n  const [recipient, setRecipient] = useState<ResolvedRecipient>(EMPTY_RECIPIENT);\n  const [amountInput, setAmountInput] = useState(\"\");\n\n  // Two-phase pending: `submitting` covers click -> wallet returns hash;\n  // useWaitForTransactionReceipt covers hash -> on-chain confirmation.\n  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);\n  const [txError, setTxError] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [txHash, setTxHash] = useState<`0x${string}` | undefined>();\n\n  const onWrongNetwork = isConnected && chainId !== USDC_CHAIN_ID;\n\n  // Live USDC balance — used for MAX and the insufficient-funds check.\n  const { data: usdcBalance, refetch: refetchBalance } = useReadContract({\n    address: USDC_ADDRESS,\n    abi: usdcAbi,\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: address ? [address] : undefined,\n    chainId: USDC_CHAIN_ID,\n    // Keep the balance fresh at a calm interval so the insufficient-funds\n    // check and MAX reflect reality (Rule 5).\n    query: { enabled: Boolean(address), refetchInterval: 5_000 },\n  });\n\n  const { writeContractAsync } = useWriteContract();\n  const {\n    isLoading: isConfirming,\n    isSuccess: isConfirmed,\n  } = useWaitForTransactionReceipt({\n    hash: txHash,\n    chainId: USDC_CHAIN_ID,\n    query: { enabled: Boolean(txHash) },\n  });\n\n  // Refetch balance once the transfer confirms and lock the button until then.\n  const [lastConfirmedHash, setLastConfirmedHash] = useState<string>();\n  if (isConfirmed && txHash && txHash !== lastConfirmedHash) {\n    setLastConfirmedHash(txHash);\n    refetchBalance();\n    setAmountInput(\"\");\n  }\n\n  const amount = useMemo(\n    () => parseAmount(amountInput, USDC_DECIMALS),\n    [amountInput],\n  );\n\n  // Insufficient-funds check against fresh on-chain balance.\n  const insufficient =\n    amount.value !== undefined &&\n    usdcBalance !== undefined &&\n    amount.value > usdcBalance;\n\n  const isSelfSend =\n    recipient.address !== undefined &&\n    address !== undefined &&\n    recipient.address.toLowerCase() === address.toLowerCase();\n\n  const amountError = amount.error\n    ? amount.error\n    : insufficient\n      ? \"Amount exceeds your USDC balance.\"\n      : null;\n\n  const amountUsd =\n    amount.value !== undefined && usdcUsd !== undefined\n      ? Number(formatUnits(amount.value, USDC_DECIMALS)) * usdcUsd\n      : null;\n\n  const formReady =\n    isConnected &&\n    !onWrongNetwork &&\n    recipient.isValid &&\n    recipient.address !== undefined &&\n    amount.value !== undefined &&\n    !amountError;\n\n  const busy = submitting || isConfirming;\n\n  const handleRecipientResolved = useCallback((r: ResolvedRecipient) => {\n    setRecipient(r);\n  }, []);\n\n  async function handleSend() {\n    if (!formReady || !recipient.address || amount.value === undefined) return;\n    setTxError(null);\n    setTxHash(undefined);\n    setLastConfirmedHash(undefined);\n    setSubmitting(true);\n    try {\n      const hash = await writeContractAsync({\n        address: USDC_ADDRESS,\n        abi: usdcAbi,\n        functionName: \"transfer\",\n        args: [recipient.address as Address, amount.value],\n        chainId: USDC_CHAIN_ID,\n      });\n      setTxHash(hash);\n    } catch (e) {\n      // finally still runs — button never locks permanently on rejection.\n      setTxError(parseTxError(e));\n    } finally {\n      setSubmitting(false);\n    }\n  }\n\n  const explorerTxUrl =\n    txHash && `${mainnet.blockExplorers.default.url}/tx/${txHash}`;\n\n  return (\n    <div className={styles.card}>\n      <AddressInput\n        value={recipientInput}\n        onChange={(v) => {\n          setRecipientInput(v);\n          // A new recipient invalidates any prior success/error banner.\n          setTxError(null);\n        }}\n        onResolvedChange={handleRecipientResolved}\n        disabled={busy}\n      />\n\n      {isSelfSend && (\n        <p className={styles.fieldHint}>\n          Heads up: this is your own connected address.\n        </p>\n      )}\n\n      {/* Amount */}\n      <div className={styles.field} style={{ marginTop: 16 }}>\n        <label className={styles.fieldLabel} htmlFor=\"amount\">\n          Amount\n        </label>\n        <div className={styles.inputWrap}>\n          <input\n            id=\"amount\"\n            className={`${styles.input} ${\n              amountError ? styles.inputInvalid : \"\"\n            }`}\n            inputMode=\"decimal\"\n            placeholder=\"0.00\"\n            autoComplete=\"off\"\n            disabled={busy}\n            value={amountInput}\n            aria-invalid={Boolean(amountError)}\n            aria-describedby=\"amount-status\"\n            onChange={(e) => {\n              setAmountInput(e.target.value);\n              setTxError(null);\n            }}\n          />\n          <div className={styles.inputSuffix}>\n            {usdcBalance !== undefined && usdcBalance > 0n && (\n              <button\n                type=\"button\"\n                className={styles.maxButton}\n                disabled={busy}\n                onClick={() =>\n                  setAmountInput(formatUnits(usdcBalance, USDC_DECIMALS))\n                }\n              >\n                MAX\n              </button>\n            )}\n            <span className={styles.unit}>{USDC_SYMBOL}</span>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n        <div id=\"amount-status\" aria-live=\"polite\">\n          {amountError ? (\n            <p className={styles.fieldError}>{amountError}</p>\n          ) : (\n            <p className={styles.fieldHint}>\n              {amountUsd !== null ? `≈ ${formatUsd(amountUsd)}` : \" \"}\n              {usdcBalance !== undefined && (\n                <>\n                  {\" \"}\n                  · Balance:{\" \"}\n                  {formatTokenAmount(usdcBalance, USDC_DECIMALS, 2)}{\" \"}\n                  {USDC_SYMBOL}\n                </>\n              )}\n            </p>\n          )}\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      {/* Review line — confirmation context before sending */}\n      {formReady && recipient.address && amount.value !== undefined && (\n        <div style={{ marginTop: 4, marginBottom: 12 }}>\n          <div className={styles.reviewRow}>\n            <span className={styles.reviewLabel}>You send</span>\n            <span className={styles.reviewValue}>\n              {formatTokenAmount(amount.value, USDC_DECIMALS, 2)} {USDC_SYMBOL}\n              {amountUsd !== null ? ` (~${formatUsd(amountUsd)})` : \"\"}\n            </span>\n          </div>\n          <div className={styles.reviewRow}>\n            <span className={styles.reviewLabel}>To</span>\n            <span className={styles.reviewValue}>\n              {recipient.ensName\n                ? `${recipient.ensName} · ${truncateAddress(recipient.address)}`\n                : truncateAddress(recipient.address)}\n            </span>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      {/* Primary action — one action at a time (Rule 2) */}\n      <PrimaryAction\n        isConnected={isConnected}\n        onWrongNetwork={onWrongNetwork}\n        isSwitching={isSwitching}\n        formReady={Boolean(formReady)}\n        submitting={submitting}\n        isConfirming={isConfirming}\n        onConnect={() => openConnectModal?.()}\n        onSwitch={() => switchChain({ chainId: USDC_CHAIN_ID })}\n        onSend={handleSend}\n      />\n\n      {/* Status feedback (Rule 7: human-readable, inline, persistent) */}\n      {txError && (\n        <div\n          className={`${styles.banner} ${styles.bannerError}`}\n          role=\"alert\"\n          style={{ marginTop: 14 }}\n        >\n          <span>{txError}</span>\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      {isConfirmed && txHash && (\n        <div\n          className={`${styles.banner} ${styles.bannerSuccess}`}\n          role=\"status\"\n          style={{ marginTop: 14 }}\n        >\n          <span>\n            Sent! Your USDC transfer confirmed on-chain.{\" \"}\n            {explorerTxUrl && (\n              <a\n                className={styles.bannerLink}\n                href={explorerTxUrl}\n                target=\"_blank\"\n                rel=\"noopener noreferrer\"\n              >\n                View on Etherscan\n              </a>\n            )}\n          </span>\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      {isConfirming && txHash && !isConfirmed && (\n        <div\n          className={`${styles.banner} ${styles.bannerSuccess}`}\n          role=\"status\"\n          style={{ marginTop: 14 }}\n        >\n          <span>\n            Transaction submitted — waiting for confirmation.{\" \"}\n            {explorerTxUrl && (\n              <a\n                className={styles.bannerLink}\n                href={explorerTxUrl}\n                target=\"_blank\"\n                rel=\"noopener noreferrer\"\n              >\n                Track it\n              </a>\n            )}\n          </span>\n        </div>\n      )}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\ninterface ActionProps {\n  isConnected: boolean;\n  onWrongNetwork: boolean;\n  isSwitching: boolean;\n  formReady: boolean;\n  submitting: boolean;\n  isConfirming: boolean;\n  onConnect: () => void;\n  onSwitch: () => void;\n  onSend: () => void;\n}\n\n// Renders exactly one primary action based on the current state.\nfunction PrimaryAction({\n  isConnected,\n  onWrongNetwork,\n  isSwitching,\n  formReady,\n  submitting,\n  isConfirming,\n  onConnect,\n  onSwitch,\n  onSend,\n}: ActionProps) {\n  if (!isConnected) {\n    return (\n      <button className={styles.button} type=\"button\" onClick={onConnect}>\n        Connect Wallet\n      </button>\n    );\n  }\n\n  if (onWrongNetwork) {\n    return (\n      <button\n        className={styles.button}\n        type=\"button\"\n        onClick={onSwitch}\n        disabled={isSwitching}\n      >\n        {isSwitching && <span className={styles.spinner} aria-hidden />}\n    type=\"button\"\n      onClick={onSend}\n      disabled={!formReady || busy}\n    >\n      {busy && <span className={styles.spinner} aria-hidden />}\n      {submitting\n        ? \"Confirm in
