error?: string } {\n  const trimmed = input.trim();\n  if (!trimmed) return {};\n  if (!/^\\d*\\.?\\d*$/.test(trimmed)) return { error: \"Enter a valid number.\" };\n  const [, fraction = \"\"] = trimmed.split(\".\");\n  if (fraction.length > decimals) {\n    return { error: `USDC supports at most ${decimals} decimals.` };\n  }\n  try {\n    const value = parseUnits(trimmed, decimals);\n    if (value <= 0n) return { error: \"Amount must be greater than 0.\" };\n    return { value };\n  } catch {\n    return { error: \"Enter a valid number.\" };\n  }\n}\n\nexport function PayForm() {\n  const { address, isConnected, chainId } = useAccount();\n  const { openConnectModal } = useConnectModal();\n  const { switchChain, isPending: isSwitching } = useSwitchChain();\n  const { usdcUsd } = usePrices();\n\n  const [recipientInput, setRecipientInput] = useState(\"\");\n  const [recipient, setRecipient] = useState<ResolvedRecipient>(EMPTY_RECIPIENT);\n  const [amountInput, setAmountInput] = useState(\"\");\n\n  // Two-phase pending: `submitting` covers click -> wallet returns hash;\n  // useWaitForTransactionReceipt covers hash -> on-chain confirmation.\n  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);\n  const [txError, setTxError] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [txHash, setTxHash] = useState<`0x${string}` | undefined>();\n\n  const onWrongNetwork = isConnected && chainId !== USDC_CHAIN_ID;\n\n  // Live USDC balance — used for MAX and the insufficient-funds check.\n  const { data: usdcBalance, refetch: refetchBalance } = useReadContract({\n    address: USDC_ADDRESS,\n    abi: usdcAbi,\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: address ? [address] : undefined,\n    chainId: USDC_CHAIN_ID,\n    // Keep the balance fresh at a calm interval so the insufficient-funds\n    // check and MAX reflect reality (Rule 5).\n    query: { enabled: Boolean(address), refetchInterval: 5_000 },\n  });\n\n  const { writeContractAsync } = useWriteContract();\n  const {\n    isLoading: isConfirming,\n    isSuccess: isConfirmed,\n  } = useWaitForTransactionReceipt({\n    hash: txHash,\n    chainId: USDC_CHAIN_ID,\n    query: { enabled: Boolean(txHash) },\n  });\n\n  // Once the transfer confirms on-chain, refresh the balance and clear the\n  // amount. Effect (not render-time) so the side effects run safely.\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (isConfirmed && txHash) {\n      refetchBalance();\n      setAmountInput(\"\");\n    }\n  }, [isConfirmed, txHash, refetchBalance]);\n\n  const amount = useMemo(\n    () => parseAmount(amountInput, USDC_DECIMALS),\n    [amountInput],\n  );\n\n  // Insufficient-funds check against fresh on-chain balance.\n  const insufficient =\n    amount.value !== undefined &&\n    usdcBalance !== undefined &&\n    amount.value > usdcBalance;\n\n  const isSelfSend =\n    recipient.address !== undefined &&\n    address !== undefined &&\n    recipient.address.toLowerCase() === address.toLowerCase();\n\n  const amountError = amount.error\n    ? amount.error\n    : insufficient\n      ? \"Amount exceeds your USDC balance.\"\n      : null;\n\n  const amountUsd =\n    amount.value !== undefined && usdcUsd !== undefined\n      ? Number(formatUnits(amount.value, USDC_DECIMALS)) * usdcUsd\n      : null;\n\n  const formReady =\n    isConnected &&\n    !onWrongNetwork &&\n    recipient.isValid &&\n    recipient.address !== undefined &&\n    amount.value !== undefined &&\n    !amountError;\n\n  const busy = submitting || isConfirming;\n\n  const handleRecipientResolved = useCallback((r: ResolvedRecipient) => {\n    setRecipient(r);\n  }, []);\n\n  async function handleSend() {\n    if (!formReady || !recipient.address || amount.value === undefined) return;\n    setTxError(null);\n    setTxHash(undefined);\n    setLastConfirmedHash(undefined);\n    setSubmitting(true);\n    try {\n      const hash = await writeContractAsync({\n        address: USDC_ADDRESS,\n        abi: usdcAbi,\n        functionName: \"transfer\",\n        args: [recipient.address as Address, amount.value],\n        chainId: USDC_CHAIN_ID,\n      });\n      setTxHash(hash);\n    } catch (e) {\n      // finally still runs — button never locks permanently on rejection.\n      setTxError(parseTxError(e));\n    } finally {\n      setSubmitting(false);\n    }\n  }\n\n  const explorerTxUrl =\n    txHash && `${mainnet.blockExplorers.default.url}/tx/${txHash}`;\n\n  return (\n    <div className={styles.card}>\n      <AddressInput\n        value={recipientInput}\n        onChange={(v) => {\n          setRecipientInput(v);\n          // A new recipient invalidates any prior success/error banner.\n          setTxError(null);\n        }}\n        onResolvedChange={handleRecipientResolved}\n        disabled={busy}\n      />\n\n      {isSelfSend && (\n        <p className={styles.fieldHint}>\n          Heads up: this is your own connected address.\n        </p>\n      )}\n\n      {/* Amount */}\n      <div className={styles.field} style={{ marginTop: 16 }}>\n        <label className={styles.fieldLabel} htmlFor=\"amount\">\n          Amount\n        </label>\n        <div className={styles.inputWrap}>\n          <input\n            id=\"amount\"\n            className={`${styles.input} ${\n              amountError ? styles.inputInvalid : \"\"\n            }`}\n            inputMode=\"decimal\"\n            placeholder=\"0.00\"\n            autoComplete=\"off\"\n            disabled={busy}\n            value={amountInput}\n            aria-invalid={Boolean(amountError)}\n            aria-describedby=\"amount-status\"\n            onChange={(e) => {\n              setAmountInput(e.target.value);\n              setTxError(null);\n            }}\n          />\n          <div className={styles.inputSuffix}>\n            {usdcBalance !== undefined && usdcBalance > 0n && (\n              <button\n                type=\"button\"\n                className={styles.maxButton}\n                disabled={busy}\n                onClick={() =>\n                  setAmountInput(formatUnits(usdcBalance, USDC_DECIMALS))\n                }\n              >\n                MAX\n              </button>\n            )}\n            <span className={styles.unit}>{USDC_SYMBOL}</span>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n        <div id=\"amount-status\" aria-live=\"polite\">\n          {amountError ? (\n            <p className={styles.fieldError}>{amountError}</p>\n          ) : (\n            <p className={styles.fieldHint}>\n              {amountUsd !== null ? `≈ ${formatUsd(amountUsd)}` : \" \"}\n              {usdcBalance !== undefined && (\n                <>\n                  {\" \"}\n                  · Balance:{\" \"}\n                  {formatTokenAmount(usdcBalance, USDC_DECIMALS, 2)}{\" \"}\n                  {USDC_SYMBOL}\n                </>\n              )}\n            </p>\n          )}\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      {/* Review line — confirmation context before sending */}\n      {formReady && recipient.address && amount.value !== undefined && (\n        <div style={{ marginTop: 4, marginBottom: 12 }}>\n          <div className={styles.reviewRow}>\n            <span className={styles.reviewLabel}>You send</span>\n            <span className={styles.reviewValue}>\n              {formatTokenAmount(amount.value, USDC_DECIMALS, 2)} {USDC_SYMBOL}\n              {amountUsd !== null ? ` (~${formatUsd(amountUsd)})` : \"\"}\n            </span>\n          </div>\n          <div className={styles.reviewRow}>\n            <span className={styles.reviewLabel}>To</span>\n            <span className={styles.reviewValue}>\n              {recipient.ensName\n                ? `${recipient.ensName} · ${truncateAddress(recipient.address)}`\n                : truncateAddress(recipient.address)}\n            </span>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      {/* Primary action — one action at a time (Rule 2) */}\n      <PrimaryAction\n        isConnected={isConnected}\n        onWrongNetwork={onWrongNetwork}\n        isSwitching={isSwitching}\n        formReady={Boolean(formReady)}\n        submitting={submitting}\n        isConfirming={isConfirming}\n        onConnect={() => openConnectModal?.()}\n        onSwitch={() => switchChain({ chainId: USDC_CHAIN_ID })}\n        onSend={handleSend}\n      />\n\n      {/* Status feedback (Rule 7: human-readable, inline, persistent) */}\n      {txError && (\n        <div\n          className={`${styles.banner} ${styles.bannerError}`}\n          role=\"alert\"\n          style={{ marginTop: 14 }}\n        >\n          <span>{txError}</span>\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      {isConfirmed && txHash && (\n        <div\n          className={`${styles.banner} ${styles.bannerSuccess}`}\n          role=\"status\"\n          style={{ marginTop: 14 }}\n        >\n          <span>\n            Sent! Your USDC transfer confirmed on-chain.{\" \"}\n            {explorerTxUrl && (\n              <a\n                className={styles.bannerLink}\n                href={explorerTxUrl}\n                target=\"_blank\"\n                rel=\"noopener noreferrer\"\n              >\n                View on Etherscan\n              </a>\n            )}\n          </span>\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      {isConfirming && txHash && !isConfirmed && (\n        <div\n          className={`${styles.banner} ${styles.bannerSuccess}`}\n          role=\"status\"\n          style={{ marginTop: 14 }}\n        >\n          <span>\n            Transaction submitted — waiting for confirmation.{\" \"}\n            {explorerTxUrl && (\n              <a\n                className={styles.bannerLink}\n                href={explorerTxUrl}\n                target=\"_blank\"\n                rel=\"noopener noreferrer\"\n              >\n                Track it\n              </a>\n            )}\n          </span>\n        </div>\n      )}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\ninterface ActionProps {\n  isConnected: boolean;\n  onWrongNetwork: boolean;\n  isSwitching: boolean;\n  formReady: boolean;\n  submitting: boolean;\n  isConfirming: boolean;\n  onConnect: () => void;\n  onSwitch: () => void;\n  onSend: () => void;\n}\n\n// Renders exactly one primary action based on the current state.\nfunction PrimaryAction({\n  isConnected,\n  onWrongNetwork,\n  isSwitching,\n  formReady,\n  submitting,\n  isConfirming,\n  onConnect,\n  onSwitch,\n  onSend,\n}: ActionProps) {\n  if (!isConnected) {\n    return (\n      <button className={styles.button} type=\"button\" onClick={onConnect}>\n        Connect Wallet\n      </button>\n    );\n  }\n\n  if (onWrongNetwork) {\n    return (\n      <button\n        className={styles.button}\n        type=\"button\"\n        onClick={onSwitch}\n        disabled={isSwitching}\n      >\n        {isSwitching && <span className={styles.spinner} aria-hidden />}\n        {isSwitching ? \"Switching…\" : \"Switch to Ethereum\"}\n      </button>\n    );\n  }\n\n  const busy = submitting || isConfirming;\n  return (\n    <button\n      className={styles.button}\n      type=\"button\"\n      onClick={onSend}\n      disabled={!formReady || busy}\n    >\n      {busy && <span className={styles.spinner} aria-hidden />}\n      {submitting\n        ? \"Confirm in );\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":146,"oldLines":7,"newStart":146,"newLines":6,"lines":["     if (!formReady || !recipient.address || amount.value === undefined) return;"," {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdLybTCZWvJhrrbMWqcy9","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Clean — 25 app files tracked, harness scaffolding excluded. The deliverable is committed and verified.\n\n## What I built\n\nA production-shaped **USDC Pay** dApp with a `/pay` page. Stack: **Next.js 14 (App Router) + TypeScript, wagmi v2 + viem, RainbowKit, TanStack Query** — the standard production stack for an Ethereum dApp.\n\nVerified: `tsc --noEmit` clean, `next build` exits 0 (`/pay` prerenders), `next lint` clean.\n\n### How it works\n- Connect wallet → enter recipient (**0x address or ENS**) → enter amount → send **real mainnet USDC** (`0xA0b8…eB48`, **6 decimals**, direct `transfer`).\n- Balances card shows live **USDC** and **ETH (gas)** balances, each with USD context; warns when ETH is too low for gas.\n\n### Frontend-UX skill rules applied\n- **Pending states (Rule 1):** send button disables on click, shows `Confirm in wallet…` → `Sending…`, stays locked through on-chain confirmation (`useWaitForTransactionReceipt`), then refetches balance. `finally` always releases, so a wallet rejection never locks it.\n- **Four-state flow (Rule 2):** Connect → Switch to Ethereum → Send, one action at a time. No Approve step — a direct transfer correctly needs none (documented in code + README).\n- **Address UX (Rule 3):** checksum validation, paste normalization, ENS forward + reverse resolution; RainbowKit's `ConnectButton` covers copy/explorer/avatar.\n- **USD context (Rule 4):** on balances, the amount input (live `≈ $`), and the review line.\n- **RPC/polling (Rule 5):** dedicated RPC via env with intentional public fallback; calm 5s balance polling.\n- **Theme (Rule 6):** semantic CSS tokens, follows system light/dark; RainbowKit modal matches.\n- **Error translation (Rule 7):** `lib/errors.ts` maps reject / insufficient-gas / contract-revert to plain sentences, shown inline near the action.\n- **Metadata (Rule 8):** OG/Twitter tags with absolute `metadataBase` URL, real favicon + OG image, product identity (no template branding).\n- **Decimals (Rule 9):** all `parseUnits`/`formatUnits` at 6 for USDC, `formatEther` for ETH.\n\n### Notes / unresolved\n- One build fix worth flagging: current `@wagmi/connectors` statically imports the Base Account connector, whose optional `@x402/*` payment modules ship no installable package. I redirect that scope to an empty stub in `next.config.mjs` (we never invoke x402). Harmless, but it's a workaround for an upstream packaging issue.\n- `.env.example` needs real values before running: `NEXT_PUBLIC_WC_PROJECT_ID` (WalletConnect) and `NEXT_PUBLIC_MAINNET_RPC_URL` (dedicated RPC). Per your instruction I did not run the dev server.\n- USD prices come from CoinGecko's public API (best-effort; the `~$` hint hides gracefully if unavailable). Swap for a keyed price feed if you want SLA guarantees."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":901,"cache_read_input_tokens":73489,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":901},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"4f67acd9-9b84-4a1a-a585-f948a9bb4cf2","uuid":"546281fb-8321-46e9-8b7a-0e0bb48686b8","timestamp":"2026-07-24T13:40:13.294Z","request_id":"req_011CdLybRStngeAFvyTt4xGP"}
